# Cube card implementation review

546 cards; 4 carry automatic review hints; 177 are named in `docs/SEARCH_LIMITATIONS.md`.

Automatic review hints are cheap text heuristics, not verdicts: an oracle line sharing no content word with the model summary, a number in the oracle text absent from the model, or a Scryfall keyword the model never mentions. Use them to decide where to read first.

## Most-flagged cards

- **Kaldra Compleat** (2): Oracle line shares no content word with the model: “Living weapon”; Scryfall keyword not in modeled keywords or model text: Living weapon
- **Balance** (1): Oracle line shares no content word with the model: “Each player chooses a number of lands they control equal to the number of lands controlled by the player who controls the fewest, then sacrifices the rest. Players discard cards and sacrifice creatures the same way.”
- **Scythecat Cub** (1): Scryfall keyword not in modeled keywords or model text: Double
- **Urza's Saga** (1): Oracle line shares no content word with the model: “I — This Saga gains "{T}: Add {C}."”

Documented shortcuts:

- *PowerLSV cube W/G creature bucket — deliberate simplifications (2026-09-27, `feature/cube-creatures-wg`)*: 5. **Batched "one or more ... enter" trigger** (Enduring Innocence) is the per-object ENTERS trigger with `once_per_turn`; the recognizer only accepts the form with "This ability triggers only once each turn.", where the two are equivalent.
- *PowerLSV cube "glue" bucket — deliberate simplifications (2026-09-27, `feature/cube-glue`)*: The last sixteen normal-layout cube cards (Chandra, Torch of Defiance; Oko, Thief of Crowns; Wrenn and Six; Ugin, Eye of the Storms; Kaito, Bane of Nightmares; Nissa, Who Shakes the World; Tamiyo, Collector of Tales; Comet, Stellar Pup; ...
